   295	    constexpr double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   296	    constexpr double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   297	    constexpr double eAB = (2.0 / 9.0);
   298	    constexpr double gamma = 0.5;
   299	    constexpr double diffusion = 1.0;
   300	    constexpr double invDx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);
   301	    constexpr double invDy2 = 1.0 / (dy * dy);
   302	    constexpr double invDz2 = 1.0 / (dz * dz);
   303	
   304	    if (rank == 0) std::printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   305	    MPI_Barrier(comm);
   306	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   307	
   308	    for (int step = 0; step < iterations; ++step) {
   309	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   310	        beginHaloExchange(cold, slab, planeSize, comm, requests);
   311	
   312	        // Planes that do not require a ghost can be computed in flight.
   313	        if (slab.localNz > 2) {
   314	            computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize, 2,
   315	                                          slab.localNz, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2,
   316	                                          gamma, eAA, eBB, eAB);
   317	        }
   318	        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   319	        if (slab.localNz != 0) {
   320	            computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize, 1, 2,
   321	                                          invDx2, invDy2, invDz2, gamma,
   322	                                          eAA, eBB, eAB);
   323	            if (slab.localNz > 1) {
   324	                computeChemicalPotentialRange(cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize,
   325	                                              slab.localNz, slab.localNz + 1,
   326	                                              invDx2, invDy2, invDz2, gamma,
   327	                                              eAA, eBB, eAB);
   328	            }
   329	        }
   330	
   331	        beginHaloExchange(mu, slab, planeSize, comm, requests);
   332	        if (slab.localNz > 2) {
   333	            updateRange(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize, 2, slab.localNz,
   334	                        dt * diffusion, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
   335	        }
   336	        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   337	        if (slab.localNz != 0) {
   338	            updateRange(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize, 1, 2,
   339	                        dt * diffusion, invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
   340	            if (slab.localNz > 1) {
   341	                updateRange(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, planeSize,
   342	                            slab.localNz, slab.localNz + 1, dt * diffusion,
   343	                            invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
   344	            }
   345	        }
   346	        cold.swap(cnew);
   347	    }
   348	
   349	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   350	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   351	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
   352	    if (rank == 0) {
   353	        const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   354	        const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations;
   355	        const double mcups = elapsed > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / elapsed / 1.0e6 : 0.0;
   356	        std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
   357	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   358	    }
   359	
   360	    if (printResults) {
   361	        bool gatherSupported = gridSize <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   362	        int gatherOk = gatherSupported ? 1 : 0;
   363	        MPI_Bcast(&gatherOk, 1, MPI_INT, 0, comm);
   364	        if (!gatherOk) {
   365	            if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Result output exceeds MPI_Gatherv limits.\n");
   366	            MPI_Finalize();
   367	            return 1;
   368	        }
   369	
   370	        std::vector<int> counts;
   371	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   372	        std::vector<double> global;
   373	        if (rank == 0) {
   374	            counts.resize(ranks);
   375	            displacements.resize(ranks);
   376	            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
   377	                const Slab other = decomposeZ(nz, r, ranks);
   378	                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(other.localNz * planeSize);
   379	                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(other.zOffset * planeSize);
   380	            }
   381	            global.resize(gridSize);
   382	        }
   383	        const int localCount = static_cast<int>(slab.localNz * planeSize);
   384	        const double* sendBuffer = slab.localNz == 0 ? nullptr : cold.data() + planeSize;
   385	        MPI_Gatherv(sendBuffer, localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
   386	                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
   387	                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   388	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   389	                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, comm);
   390	        if (rank == 0) print_results(global, "Concentration");
   391	    }
   392	
   393	    bool valid = true;
   394	    if (validate) {
   395	        if (rank == 0) std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   396	        valid = validateResult(cold, slab, planeSize, comm, rank);
   397	        if (rank == 0) std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   398	    }
   399	
   400	    MPI_Finalize();
   401	    return valid ? 0 : 1;
   402	}

-// 3D index calculation
-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+namespace {
+
+struct Slab {
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t zOffset;
+    int lowerRank;
+    int upperRank;
+};
+
+Slab decomposeZ(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t zOffset = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const int activeRanks = static_cast<int>(std::min(nz, static_cast<size_t>(ranks)));
+
+    return {localNz,
+            zOffset,
+            localNz != 0 && rank > 0 ? rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL,
+            localNz != 0 && rank + 1 < activeRanks ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL};
 }

-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {

-    
+inline size_t index3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                     const size_t nx, const size_t planeSize) noexcept {
+    return z * planeSize + y * nx + x;
+}
+
+// Begin a halo exchange. Physical z boundaries are filled by copying the
+// adjacent plane, which exactly implements the original clamped boundary.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       const size_t planeSize, MPI_Comm comm,
+                       MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    int count = 0;
+    if (slab.localNz == 0) {
+        requests[0] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[1] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[2] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[3] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (slab.lowerRank == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(field.data() + planeSize, planeSize, field.data());
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.lowerRank, 101, comm, &requests[count++]);
+        MPI_Isend(field.data() + planeSize, static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.lowerRank, 102, comm, &requests[count++]);
+    }
+
+    if (slab.upperRank == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(field.data() + slab.localNz * planeSize, planeSize,
+                    field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * planeSize);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * planeSize,
+                  static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.upperRank, 102, comm, &requests[count++]);
+        MPI_Isend(field.data() + slab.localNz * planeSize,
+                  static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.upperRank, 101, comm, &requests[count++]);
+    }
+
+    while (count < 4) requests[count++] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+}
+
+inline double laplacianAt(const double* const field, const size_t x,
+                          const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx,
+                          const size_t ny, const size_t planeSize,
+                          const double invDx2, const double invDy2,
+                          const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const size_t center = index3(x, y, z, nx, planeSize);
+    const size_t left = x == 0 ? center : center - 1;
+    const size_t right = x + 1 == nx ? center : center + 1;
+    const size_t down = y == 0 ? center : center - nx;
+    const size_t up = y + 1 == ny ? center : center + nx;
+
+    const double cxx = (field[right] + field[left] - 2.0 * field[center]) * invDx2;
+    const double cyy = (field[up] + field[down] - 2.0 * field[center]) * invDy2;
+    const double czz = (field[center + planeSize] + field[center - planeSize] -
+                        2.0 * field[center]) * invDz2;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }

codex
Implemented mandatory MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cahn_hilliard` executable name.

Key changes:

- Balanced z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- Nonblocking halo exchanges overlapped with interior computation.
- Correct clamped physical boundaries.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Global maximum runtime used for performance reporting.
- Global result gathering only for `-r`.
- Handles more MPI ranks than z-planes.
- MPI is now required by [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:8).

Validation completed successfully:

- Clean CMake build with no warnings.
- Identical result hashes between the original serial solver and MPI runs.
- Tested with 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks.
- Tested degenerate dimensions, zero iterations, validation, help, and invalid input.
- No new repository files created.

Example:

```bash
cmake -S cahn-hilliard -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpirun -np 8 ./build/cahn_hilliard -x 128 -i 20 -v
```

-// 3D index calculation
-inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
-    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
+namespace {
+
+struct Slab {
+    size_t localNz;
+    size_t zOffset;
+    int lowerRank;
+    int upperRank;
+};
+
+Slab decomposeZ(const size_t nz, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = nz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t zOffset = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const int activeRanks = static_cast<int>(std::min(nz, static_cast<size_t>(ranks)));
+
+    return {localNz,
+            zOffset,
+            localNz != 0 && rank > 0 ? rank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL,
+            localNz != 0 && rank + 1 < activeRanks ? rank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL};
 }

-double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
-    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
-    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;

-                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);

-    
+inline size_t index3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                     const size_t nx, const size_t planeSize) noexcept {
+    return z * planeSize + y * nx + x;
+}
+
+// Begin a halo exchange. Physical z boundaries are filled by copying the
+// adjacent plane, which exactly implements the original clamped boundary.
+void beginHaloExchange(std::vector<double>& field, const Slab& slab,
+                       const size_t planeSize, MPI_Comm comm,
+                       MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    int count = 0;
+    if (slab.localNz == 0) {
+        requests[0] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[1] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[2] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        requests[3] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (slab.lowerRank == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(field.data() + planeSize, planeSize, field.data());
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data(), static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.lowerRank, 101, comm, &requests[count++]);
+        MPI_Isend(field.data() + planeSize, static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.lowerRank, 102, comm, &requests[count++]);
+    }
+
+    if (slab.upperRank == MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        std::copy_n(field.data() + slab.localNz * planeSize, planeSize,
+                    field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * planeSize);
+    } else {
+        MPI_Irecv(field.data() + (slab.localNz + 1) * planeSize,
+                  static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.upperRank, 102, comm, &requests[count++]);
+        MPI_Isend(field.data() + slab.localNz * planeSize,
+                  static_cast<int>(planeSize), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                  slab.upperRank, 101, comm, &requests[count++]);
+    }
+
+    while (count < 4) requests[count++] = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;
+}
+
+inline double laplacianAt(const double* const field, const size_t x,
+                          const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx,
+                          const size_t ny, const size_t planeSize,
+                          const double invDx2, const double invDy2,
+                          const double invDz2) noexcept {
+    const size_t center = index3(x, y, z, nx, planeSize);
+    const size_t left = x == 0 ? center : center - 1;
+    const size_t right = x + 1 == nx ? center : center + 1;
+    const size_t down = y == 0 ? center : center - nx;
+    const size_t up = y + 1 == ny ? center : center + nx;
+
+    const double cxx = (field[right] + field[left] - 2.0 * field[center]) * invDx2;
+    const double cyy = (field[up] + field[down] - 2.0 * field[center]) * invDy2;
+    const double czz = (field[center + planeSize] + field[center - planeSize] -
+                        2.0 * field[center]) * invDz2;
     return cxx + cyy + czz;
 }

-                const double cv = c[idx];

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+                const size_t index = index3(x, y, z, nx, planeSize);
+                const double cv = cData[index];
+                muData[index] =
+                    4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * eAA + (cv - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * cv * eAB) +
+                    3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv -
+                    gamma * laplacianAt(cData, x, y, z, nx, ny, planeSize,

             }

 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+void updateRange(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
+                 const std::vector<double>& mu, const size_t nx,
+                 const size_t ny, const size_t planeSize,
+                 const size_t zBegin, const size_t zEnd, const double dtD,
+                 const double invDx2, const double invDy2,
+                 const double invDz2) {
+    double* const newData = cnew.data();
+    const double* const oldData = cold.data();
+    const double* const muData = mu.data();
+    for (size_t z = zBegin; z < zEnd; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
             for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+                const size_t index = index3(x, y, z, nx, planeSize);
+                newData[index] = oldData[index] +
+                    dtD * laplacianAt(muData, x, y, z, nx, ny, planeSize,
+                                      invDx2, invDy2, invDz2);
             }
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx,

+                             const Slab& slab, const size_t planeSize) {
+    const size_t volume = nx * ny * nz;
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= slab.localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = slab.zOffset + localZ - 1;
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
             for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

             }
         }
     }
 }

tokens used
57,910
Implemented mandatory MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cahn_hilliard` executable name.

Key changes:

- Balanced z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- Nonblocking halo exchanges overlapped with interior computation.
- Correct clamped physical boundaries.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Global maximum runtime used for performance reporting.
- Global result gathering only for `-r`.
- Handles more MPI ranks than z-planes.
- MPI is now required by [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:8).

Validation completed successfully:

- Clean CMake build with no warnings.
- Identical result hashes between the original serial solver and MPI runs.
- Tested with 1, 2, 4, and 8 ranks.
- Tested degenerate dimensions, zero iterations, validation, help, and invalid input.
- No new repository files created.

Example:

```bash
cmake -S cahn-hilliard -B build
cmake --build build -j
mpirun -np 8 ./build/cahn_hilliard -x 128 -i 20 -v
```
